\displaystyle=({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{4}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}\right]\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\right]\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{4}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}

\displaystyle=({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\left(\frac{\rho^{4}\eta^{4}}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}+o(1)\right)(1-c)^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{4}}\frac{c^{2}}{(1-c)^{3}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{4}}\right)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{4}}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

and

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{3}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\xi}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right]\right)^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right]+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Now we have the expectations and errors for the three terms. Combining them yields the Lemma statement.

For
c>1
, we recall that
{\bm{h}}{\bm{s}}=\bm{0}
, and Proposition
1
implies

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}(\xi^{2}+\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2})}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}\right)({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}\right)({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}.

Hence, we can take expectation:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{Z}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\right]({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{c-1}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

∎

Lemma 27
.

In the same setting as
Section

2
, we have that,

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\begin{cases}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}-\frac{2\eta^{2}c}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)&c<1\\
\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{c}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\left(\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}-\frac{({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}}{c(c-1)}\right)+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)&c>1\end{cases}.

Proof.

We use similar expansions that follow from Lemma
2
.

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{4}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}({\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}})({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}

\displaystyle+\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\xi}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.

Lemma
26
gives the expectation of the first four terms:

\displaystyle\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{2}\|^{2}+\frac{\eta^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

We have done the following expectations in Equations
18
,
D.5.7
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Combining these results yields the lemma statement.

For
c>1
, with
{\bm{h}}{\bm{s}}=\bm{0}
,
{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}=\bm{0}
,
{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}={\bm{h}}
, we have the following expansion by Lemma
2
:

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle={\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}

\displaystyle-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.

We can combine the coefficients as:

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}=\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}(\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}+\xi^{2})-2\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}=-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}},

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}=\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}(\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}+\xi^{2})}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}=\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\gamma_{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}=\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}.

Then we have that:

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}.

Recall from Equation
13
that
\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}]=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}/c
. We then proceed similarly with the other expectations using Lemmas
9
,
10
,
11
,
19
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\rho^{2}\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(1-\frac{1}{c}\right)\left(\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}\frac{c}{\rho^{2}(c-1)}+o\left(\frac{1}{d\rho^{2}}\right)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\rho^{2}\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{c-1}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{c^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o\left(1\right)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\frac{({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}}{c(c-1)}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}

\displaystyle=0+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

(17)

We combine these results to produce the lemma statement.
∎

Lemma 28
.

In the same setting as
Section

2
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\begin{cases}-\left(\frac{\eta^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}-\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}\right)({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),&c<1\\
-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\frac{1}{c-1}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right),&c>1\end{cases}

Proof.

For
c<1
, we expand it using
1
,
Lemma

2
. Note that all of the relevant expectations have been evaluated in the proofs of Lemmas
26
,
27
,

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\xi}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}

\displaystyle-\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{4}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}({\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}})({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}.

The expectation of the last three terms is given by Lemma
26
. The first two expectations come from Equations
18
,
D.5.7
respectively. We can plug them in and compute the expectation:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=-\left(\frac{\eta^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}-\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}\right)({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

For
c>1
, again with
{\bm{h}}{\bm{s}}=\bm{0}
and
{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}=\bm{0}
,
{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}
becomes:

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}+\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{s}}{\bm{h}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}-\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}\right){\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle+{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}+\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{s}}{\bm{h}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}-\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}\right){\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}-\frac{\eta^{3}\xi\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}\right]{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle\ +{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left[\frac{\eta^{3}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\xi}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}-\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}\right]{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}-\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}\right]{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})\left(\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)\quad\text{since $\gamma_{2}=\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}+\xi^{2}$}.

We need to evaluate two following expectations. Similar to
c<1
,

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\rho^{2}\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{c-1}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\frac{({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})}{c-1}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Finally, we have that:

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\frac{1}{c-1}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

∎

Lemma 29
.

In the same setting as
Section

2
, we have that,

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]=\begin{cases}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{cd}{\rho^{2}(1-c)}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}\right)+o\left(\frac{n}{\rho^{2}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}n}\right),&c<1\\
\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{d}{\rho^{2}(c-1)}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}\frac{c}{c-1}\right)+o\left(\frac{n}{\rho^{2}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}n}\right),&c>1\end{cases}

Proof.

For
c<1
, we first expand this term using Theorem
6
:

\displaystyle{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({{\bm{Z}}}+{{\bm{A}}})^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}=\

\displaystyle{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}\left({{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}+\frac{\eta}{{\xi}}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}-\frac{{\xi}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{p}}_{1}{\bm{q}}_{1}^{\top}\right)\left({{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}+\frac{\eta}{{\xi}}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}-\frac{{\xi}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{p}}_{1}{\bm{q}}_{1}^{\top}\right)^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}

\displaystyle=\

\displaystyle{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}+\frac{2\eta}{{\xi}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}{{\bm{t}}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}-\frac{2{\xi}}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{\bm{q}}_{1}{\bm{p}}_{1}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}

\displaystyle+\frac{\eta^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}\left({{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}\right){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{\bm{q}}_{1}{\bm{p}}_{1}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}+\frac{{\xi}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{\bm{p}}_{1}{\bm{q}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{q}}_{1}{\bm{p}}_{1}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}

Note that Lemma
21
and the fact that
{\bm{t}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}=\bm{0}
imply that the second term has zero expectation:

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\left[\frac{2\eta}{{\xi}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}{{\bm{t}}}{\varepsilon}\right]=\frac{2\eta\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{{\xi}}{\bm{t}}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}=0.

Simiarly, we will later use:

\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\left[{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}{\bm{t}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}{\bm{t}}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}=0,\quad\mathbb{E}_{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\left[{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{\bm{t}}^{\top}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}Tr({\bm{t}}^{\top}{\bm{k}}^{\top})=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}Tr({\bm{k}}{\bm{t}})=0.

Note that these equalities are exact without taking the expectation over other sources of randomness besides
{\bm{\varepsilon}}
.

We now expand the other terms one by one and compute their expectations along the way. We start by eliminating zero expectations and taking expectations w.r.t.
{\bm{\varepsilon}}
using Lemma
21
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}\left({{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}\right){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{{\xi}^{2}}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}\right].

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2{\xi}}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{\bm{q}}_{1}{\bm{p}}_{1}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2{\xi}}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}\left(\frac{\eta\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}}{{\xi}}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}+{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{{\xi}}{{\bm{t}}}+\eta{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}\right){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}{{\bm{t}}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \left.\quad\quad-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}-\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}-\frac{2\eta\xi\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}\right].

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{\bm{q}}_{1}{\bm{p}}_{1}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma_{1}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}\left(\frac{\eta\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}}{{\xi}}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}+{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{{\xi}}{{\bm{t}}}+\eta{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}\right){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{4}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}^{2}}\left({{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}\right){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}}({{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right.

\displaystyle\ \ \left.\quad\quad-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}}\left({{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}\right){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}-\frac{2\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}({{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}){{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{{\bm{t}}}^{\top}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\ \mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{4}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{4}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}^{2}}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger\top}{{\bm{k}}}-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}}{{\bm{k}}}^{\top}{{\bm{A}}}^{\dagger}{{\bm{h}}}^{\top}\right].

By the squared norms in Lemmas
6
,
7
, and Lemma
21
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\xi}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{\bm{p}}_{1}{\bm{q}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{q}}_{1}{\bm{p}}_{1}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{{\xi}^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}\|{\bm{p}}_{1}\|^{2}\|{\bm{q}}_{1}\|^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{{\xi}^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}^{2}}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\gamma_{1}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{4}}{\xi^{2}}{\bm{k}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}+\frac{2\eta\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\left(\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{4}}{\xi^{2}}{\bm{k}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}+\frac{2\eta\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}\xi^{2}}{\bm{k}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}+\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}\xi}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right)

We combine like terms and simplify the coefficients. For the term
{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}
,

\displaystyle\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}\xi^{2}}-\frac{2\eta^{4}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{4}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}\xi^{2}}-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2}{\gamma_{1}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\left(-\frac{\gamma_{1}-\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2}{\gamma_{1}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\left(-\frac{\gamma_{1}-\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{1}\xi^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{1}}{\gamma_{1}\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}.

For the term
{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}
,

\displaystyle\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}\xi}-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{{\bm{k}}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{t}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}{\xi}}-\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}\right)=-\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}.

Combining these terms together, we have:

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}k^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}-\frac{2\eta\xi\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right].

Similarly, using Lemmas
9
,
10
,
11
,
19
,
21
, we have the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[Tr({\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top})\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\lambda}\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{cd}{\rho^{2}(1-c)}+o\left(\frac{d}{\rho^{2}}\right)\quad\text{by Equation \ref{eq:inverse_eig}}.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\rho^{2}\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)(1-c)\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{4}}\frac{c^{2}}{(1-c)^{3}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{4}}\right)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{\rho^{2}(1-c)^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{h}}\|\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right)O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\rho^{2}\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{1-c}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}\frac{c^{3}}{\rho^{2}(1-c)^{2}}+o\left(1\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

After simple algebra, the result follows from here.

For
c>1
, we can expand similarly using Theorem
6
,

\displaystyle{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle={\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}\left({\bm{A}}^{\dagger}+\frac{\eta}{\xi}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}-\frac{\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{p}}_{2}{\bm{q}}_{2}^{\top}\right)\left({\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}+\frac{\eta}{\xi}{\bm{s}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}-\frac{\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{q}}_{2}{\bm{p}}_{2}^{\top}\right){\bm{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle={\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}+\frac{2\eta}{\xi}\,{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}\underbrace{{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{s}}}_{0}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}-\frac{2\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{q}}_{2}{\bm{p}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}

\displaystyle\quad+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\,{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{q}}_{2}{\bm{p}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}+\frac{\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{p}}_{2}{\bm{q}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{q}}_{2}{\bm{p}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}.

We expand the other terms one by one, marking those with zero expectations:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\,{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\xi^{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right].

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{q}}_{2}{\bm{p}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}\left(\frac{\eta\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{s}}+{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}+\eta{\bm{k}}^{\top}\right){\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}+\eta{\bm{k}}^{\top}\right){\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}-\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}-\frac{2\eta\xi\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right].

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{q}}_{2}{\bm{p}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}\left(\frac{\eta\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{s}}+{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}+\eta{\bm{k}}^{\top}\right){\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\left(\frac{\eta\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}+\eta{\bm{k}}^{\top}\right){\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi^{2}}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi}{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[-\frac{2\eta^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi^{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right].

Using the squared norms from Lemmas
6
,
7
,

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\xi}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}^{\top}{\bm{p}}_{2}{\bm{q}}_{2}^{\top}{\bm{q}}_{2}{\bm{p}}_{2}^{\top}{{\bm{\varepsilon}}}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\xi}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\|{\bm{p}}_{2}\|^{2}\|{\bm{q}}_{2}\|^{2}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{{\xi}^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}^{2}}\left(\frac{\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\gamma_{2}\right)\left(\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{4}}{\xi^{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\right)\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[{\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}\left(\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{4}}{\gamma_{2}\xi^{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right)\right].

Similarly, we combine the coefficients: For the term
{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}
,

\displaystyle\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{\eta^{4}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{4}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi^{2}}-\frac{2\eta^{4}\|{{\bm{s}}}\|^{4}\|{{\bm{h}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{s}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi^{2}}-\frac{2\eta^{2}\|{{\bm{s}}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{h}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2}{\gamma_{2}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\left(-\frac{\gamma_{2}-\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2}{\gamma_{2}}+\frac{1}{\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\left(-\frac{\gamma_{2}-\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}{\xi}^{2}}-\frac{2\xi^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi^{2}}+\frac{\gamma_{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi^{2}}\right)

\displaystyle=-\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}.

For the term
{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}
,

\displaystyle\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}\xi}-\frac{2\eta^{3}\|{{\bm{s}}}\|^{2}\|{{\bm{h}}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}{\xi}}-\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}\right)=-\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{2\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}.

Combining these terms together, we have:

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}-\frac{2\eta\xi\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right].

Similarly, replicating the proof with the
c>1
counterparts, we have the following:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\varepsilon}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{\varepsilon}}\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\mathbb{E}\left[Tr({\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top})\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}n\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{1}{\lambda}\right]=\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\frac{d}{\rho^{2}(c-1)}+o\left(\frac{d}{\rho^{2}}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right]=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\frac{c^{2}}{\rho^{2}(c-1)^{2}}+o\left(1\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{k}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{h}}^{\top}\right]=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{k}}\|^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right]=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{\rho^{2}(c-1)^{2}}+o\left(1\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

After simple algebra, the result follows.

∎

D.5.7
Target Alignment: Helper Lemmas

Lemma 30
.

In the same setting as
Section

2
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\begin{cases}\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\rho^{2}+\eta^{2}c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)&c<1\\
\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)&c>1\end{cases}.

Proof.

For
c<1
, from
1
, we get that

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}=\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.

To begin, we start estimating

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right].

Using our Spherical Hypercontractivity, we have that
\frac{\xi}{\eta}
and
{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}
satisfy the assumptions for Lemma
36
. Then using Lemma
9
we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}\right]=\frac{1}{\eta}\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{1}{\eta}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)

and Lemma
11
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=0\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)

Thus, using Lemma
37
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=0+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)

and using Lemma
36
, since all the means are
O(1)
, we have that

\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\max\left(\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\xi}{\eta}\right),\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)\right)\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}n}\right).

Then Lemma
19
gives mean and variance of
\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{i}}
. Since
\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{i}}
does not satisfy the higher moment bound, and cannot be directly included in the product, we can include it via the classical bound:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]+\sqrt{\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right)}=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

(18)

For the second term, we begin with

\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right].

Lemma
9
tells us that

\mathbb{E}[\|t\|^{2}]=1-c\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

and Lemma
10
tells us

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{1-c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\quad\text{ and }\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{4}d}\right).

Thus using Lemmas
37
and Lemma
36
, we get that

\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\frac{c}{\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

Recalling the mean and variance for
\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}
from
19
, we have that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{t}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger\top}{\bm{k}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]+\sqrt{O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{1}}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\rho^{2}\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\frac{c}{\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

(19)

Combining these two terms yields the first result.

Similarly, for
c>1
,
1
gives the expansion:

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{Z}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle={\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left(\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}=\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}+\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.

For the first term, we begin with

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right].

Recalling form Lemma
11
, we see that

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=0\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right).

Thus again using Lemma
36
and Lemma
37
, we see that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=0+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right).

Next using the standard covariance bound on the expectation of the product. We see that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{1}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=0+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

For the second term, we begin with

\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right].

Recall from Lemma
9
we have that

\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}]=\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{c-1}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}(\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2})=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{4}n}\right)

and from Lemma
10

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\left(1-\frac{1}{c}\right)({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

Thus using Lemma
36
and Lemma
37
, we get that

\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}}{\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

Recalling the mean and variance for
\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}
from Lemma
19
and using the classical covariance bound for the expectation of the product, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right]\mathbb{E}\left[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{s}}{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]+\sqrt{O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}\right)}

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{\rho^{2}\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}}{\rho^{2}}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Then adding the two together, we get the result for
c>1
as well.
∎

Lemma 31
.

In the same setting as
Section

2
, we have that, for
c<1

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\rho^{2}+\eta^{2}c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

and for
c>1

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{1}{c}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\left(\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}+\frac{1}{c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right)+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Proof.

For
c<1
, using the expectation from Lemma
30
, we get

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left({\bm{I}}-{\bm{Z}}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\rho^{2}+\eta^{2}c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o\left(1\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

For
c>1
, using
Lemma

2
, we get

\displaystyle{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}

\displaystyle={\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left({\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}+\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}-\frac{\eta^{2}\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}-\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}\right)^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}.

We then compute the expectation of each term above. To begin, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{1}{c}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}\quad\text{by Equation \ref{eq:bAAb}}.

Next, we recall from Lemma
11
that

\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}]=0\text{ and }\mathrm{Var}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*})=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right).

and from Lemma
9
that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}\right]=\frac{1}{\eta}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\xi}{\eta}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}n}\right)

Thus, using Lemmas
36
and Lemma
37
, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}n}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}n}\right).

Then recalling the mean and variance of
\eta^{2}/\gamma_{2}
from
19
, using the standard covariance bound on the difference between the product of the expectation and the expectation of the product, we get that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\text{ and }\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta\xi}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Furthermore, for the next three terms, recall from Lemma
10
that

\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}]=\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}\frac{c}{\rho^{2}(c-1)}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d^{2}}\right)

and

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{c-1}{c^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\frac{1}{d}\right)

and from Lemma
11

\mathbb{E}[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}]=0\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d^{2}}\right).

Then recalling from Lemma
9
, we have that

\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}]=1-\frac{1}{c}\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}(\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2})=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

Then using Lemma
36
and Lemma
37
, we have that for third term

\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}]=\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)

for the fourth term

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}]

\displaystyle=\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{c-1}+o\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\right)\right)\left(\frac{c-1}{c^{2}}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+o(1)\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}}{\rho^{2}c}+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)

with variance

\mathrm{Var}(\|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top})=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right).

For the first term, we have that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=0+O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\right)

Adding the last three terms and using Lemma
34
twice, we get that

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left(\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}+|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\top{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}+\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}\right){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{\rho^{2}d}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+\frac{({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}}{\rho^{2}c}+0+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)

With variance

\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left(\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}+|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\top{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}+\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}\right){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)

Then recalling the mean and variance of
\eta^{2}/\gamma_{2}
from Lemma
19
, and using the covariance bound for the expectation of products, we get that

\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{\eta^{2}}{\gamma_{2}}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}\left(\|{\bm{s}}\|^{2}{\bm{h}}^{\top}{\bm{h}}+|{\bm{h}}\|^{2}\top{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{s}}^{\top}+\frac{\xi}{\eta}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{u}}{\bm{h}}\right){\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\left(\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}+\frac{1}{c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right)+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

Adding all five terms, we get that

\mathbb{E}\left[{\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}({\bm{Z}}+{\bm{A}})^{\dagger\top}{\bm{A}}^{\top}{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\right]=\frac{1}{c}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\left(\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}+\frac{1}{c}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right)+o(1)+O\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

∎

D.6
Step 5: Upscaling and Asymptotic Risk Formulas

In the previous step we derived downscaled expressions for the four constituent terms of the risk:
Bias, Variance, Data Noise, and Target Alignment
. We stop our abuse of notation and are explicit again about douwnscaled vs. upscaled.

Bias (downscaled).

For
c<1
, the bias term is

\frac{\tilde{\eta}^{2}}{\tilde{n}}\!\left(\Big[(\tilde{\alpha}_{Z}-\alpha_{Z})+\frac{\rho^{2}}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}(\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})\Big]^{2}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}\,\frac{c}{1-c}\,\frac{1}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}\right)+o\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{n}}\right)+o\left(\frac{1}{n}\right).

For
c>1
, the bias term is

\begin{split}\frac{\tilde{\eta}^{2}}{\tilde{n}}\!\left[({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\!\left((\tilde{\alpha}_{Z}-\alpha_{Z})+\frac{\rho^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\Big(\alpha_{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\Big)\right)^{\!2}+\alpha_{A}^{2}\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}\Big(\tfrac{c-1}{c}\Big)\frac{\eta^{2}\rho^{2}}{(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})^{2}}\right.\quad\quad\\
\quad\quad\left.+\frac{\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}}{c-1}\,\frac{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}{(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})^{2}}\right].+o\left(\frac{1}{\tilde{n}}\right)+o\left(\frac{1}{n}\right)\end{split}

Variance (downscaled).

For
c<1
, the variance term is

\begin{split}\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{2}}{d}\!\left[\alpha_{A}^{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\!\left((\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})^{2}\frac{\eta^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}\,\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}+2\alpha_{A}(\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})\,\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}\right)\right.\quad\quad\\
\quad\quad\left.+\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}\!\left(\frac{c}{1-c}\frac{d}{\rho^{2}}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})}\,\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}\right)\right].\end{split}

For
c>1
, the variance term is

\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{2}}{d}\!\left[\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}\!\left(\frac{\alpha_{A}^{2}}{c}-\frac{\alpha_{A}^{2}}{d}\,\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\right)+({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\,\frac{c}{c-1}\,\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\,\Big(\alpha_{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\Big)^{2}+\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}\!\left(\frac{d}{\rho^{2}}\frac{1}{c-1}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}\,\frac{c}{c-1}\right)\right].

Data noise (downscaled).

The data noise term is

\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{A}^{2}\,\tilde{\rho}^{2}}{d}\,\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}.

Target alignment (downscaled).

For
c<1
, the alignment term is

-\frac{2\tilde{\alpha}_{A}\tilde{\rho}^{2}}{d}\left(\alpha_{A}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+(\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})\,({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\,\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\rho^{2}+\eta^{2}c}\right).

For
c>1
, the alignment term is

-\frac{2\tilde{\alpha}_{A}\tilde{\rho}^{2}}{d}\left(\frac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha_{A}}{d}\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\,\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+\Big(\alpha_{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\Big)\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\,({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right).

These formulas are expressed in terms of the concentrated building blocks, but still at the “microscopic” scale in which
\eta
is
O(\sqrt{d})
,
\rho=\Theta(1)
, and
\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}=O(1/d)
.

In this section we return to the macroscopic, or upscaled, version of the problem. Specifically, we multiply each term by
d
and reparametrize according to

\theta^{2}\;=\;\frac{d}{n}\eta^{2},\qquad\tilde{\theta}^{2}\;=\;\frac{d}{\tilde{n}}\tilde{\eta}^{2},\qquad\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\;=\;d\,\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2},

while keeping
\rho,\tilde{\rho}
fixed. This normalization ensures that the effective spike strength
\theta
, isotropic noise level
\rho
, and label noise
\tau_{\varepsilon,r}
are all of order one. In this scaling, the risk is
d
times larger than in the downscaled representation, and the resulting formulas cleanly separate the contributions of the four terms.

The terms change as follows

Front factors (after multiplying by
d
).

\displaystyle\frac{\tilde{\eta}^{2}}{\tilde{n}}\ \xrightarrow{\ \times d\ }\ \tilde{\theta}^{2},\qquad\frac{\tilde{\rho}^{2}}{d}\ \xrightarrow{\ \times d\ }\ \tilde{\rho}^{2},\qquad\frac{\tilde{\alpha}_{A}^{2}\,\tilde{\rho}^{2}}{d}\ \xrightarrow{\ \times d\ }\ \tilde{\alpha}_{A}^{2}\,\tilde{\rho}^{2},\qquad d\,\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}\ \to\ \tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}.

(20)

Denominator identities.

\displaystyle\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}\;=\;\theta^{2}+\rho^{2},\qquad\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}\;=\;\frac{\theta^{2}+c\,\rho^{2}}{c}.

(21)

Frequently used ratios and their upscaled forms.

\displaystyle\frac{\rho^{2}}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\rho^{2}}{\theta^{2}+\rho^{2}},

(22)

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+\rho^{2}},

(23)

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+c\,\rho^{2}},

(24)

\displaystyle\frac{\rho^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{c\,\rho^{2}}{\theta^{2}+c\,\rho^{2}},

(25)

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{2}\,\rho^{2}}{(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})^{2}}

\displaystyle=\frac{\theta^{2}\,\rho^{2}}{(\theta^{2}+c\,\rho^{2})^{2}}\;c,

(26)

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}{(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})^{2}}\,\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}

\displaystyle=\frac{\theta^{2}(\theta^{2}+c\,\rho^{2})}{(\theta^{2}+\rho^{2})^{2}}\,\frac{1}{1-c}.

(27)

Noise terms with aspect-ratio factors.

After multiplying by
d
and substituting
\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}=d\,\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}
:

\displaystyle\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}\left(\frac{c}{1-c}\frac{d}{\rho^{2}}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\eta^{2}c+\rho^{2})}\frac{c^{2}}{1-c}\right)\ \longrightarrow\ \tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{c}{1-c}-\frac{\theta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\theta^{2}+\rho^{2})}\frac{c}{1-c}\right),

(28)

\displaystyle\tau_{\varepsilon,r}^{2}\left(\frac{d}{\rho^{2}}\frac{1}{c-1}-\frac{\eta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\eta^{2}+\rho^{2})}\frac{c}{c-1}\right)\ \longrightarrow\ \tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\frac{1}{c-1}-\frac{\theta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\theta^{2}+c\,\rho^{2})}\frac{c}{c-1}\right).

(29)

Alignment-specific identities.

\displaystyle\frac{\eta^{2}c}{\rho^{2}+\eta^{2}c}

\displaystyle=\frac{\theta^{2}}{\rho^{2}+\theta^{2}},\qquad\frac{\eta^{2}}{\eta^{2}+\rho^{2}}=\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+c\,\rho^{2}}.

(30)

We now state the explicit upscaled limits for each component. As before, we present results separately in the underparametrized regime (
c<1
) and the overparametrized regime (
c>1
). Each term has a little
o(1)
error term.

Bias.

For
c<1
, the bias contribution is

\tilde{\theta}^{2}\!\left(\Big[(\tilde{\alpha}_{Z}-\alpha_{Z})+\frac{\rho^{2}}{\theta^{2}+\rho^{2}}(\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})\Big]^{2}({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}+\frac{\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{d}\,\frac{c}{1-c}\,\frac{1}{\theta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\right).

For
c>1
, the bias is

\tilde{\theta}^{2}\!\left[({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\!\left((\tilde{\alpha}_{Z}-\alpha_{Z})+\frac{\rho^{2}}{\tfrac{\theta^{2}}{c}+\rho^{2}}\Big(\alpha_{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\Big)\right)^{\!2}+\alpha_{A}^{2}\frac{\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}}{d}\left(\frac{c-1}{c}\right)\frac{\tfrac{\theta^{2}}{c}\rho^{2}}{\big(\tfrac{\theta^{2}}{c}+\rho^{2}\big)^{2}}+\frac{\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}}{d}\,\frac{1}{c-1}\,\frac{\theta^{2}+\rho^{2}}{\big(\tfrac{\theta^{2}}{c}+\rho^{2}\big)^{2}}\right].

Variance.

For
c<1
, the variance contribution is

\begin{split}\tilde{\rho}^{2}\!\left[\alpha_{A}^{2}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\!\left((\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})^{2}\,\frac{\theta^{2}(\theta^{2}+c\rho^{2})}{(\theta^{2}+\rho^{2})^{2}}\,\frac{1}{1-c}+2\alpha_{A}(\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})\,\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+\rho^{2}}\right)\right.\\
\left.+\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\!\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\,\frac{c}{1-c}-\frac{1}{d}\,\frac{\theta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\theta^{2}+\rho^{2})}\cdot\frac{c}{1-c}\right)\right].\end{split}

For
c>1
, the variance is

\tilde{\rho}^{2}\!\left[\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}\!\left(\frac{\alpha_{A}^{2}}{c}-\frac{\alpha_{A}^{2}}{d}\,\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+c\rho^{2}}\right)+({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\,\frac{c}{c-1}\,\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+c\rho^{2}}\Big(\alpha_{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\Big)^{\!2}+\tau_{\varepsilon}^{2}\!\left(\frac{1}{\rho^{2}}\,\frac{1}{c-1}-\frac{1}{d}\,\frac{\theta^{2}}{\rho^{2}(\theta^{2}+c\rho^{2})}\cdot\frac{c}{c-1}\right)\right].

Data Noise.

The data noise term is independent of
c
:

\tilde{\alpha}_{A}^{2}\,\tilde{\rho}^{2}\,\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}.

Target Alignment.

For
c<1
, the target alignment contribution is

-2\tilde{\alpha}_{A}\tilde{\rho}^{2}\left(\alpha_{A}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+(\alpha_{Z}-\alpha_{A})\,({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\,\frac{\theta^{2}}{\rho^{2}+\theta^{2}}\right).

For
c>1
, the alignment term is

-2\tilde{\alpha}_{A}\tilde{\rho}^{2}\left(\frac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}-\frac{\alpha_{A}}{d}\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+c\rho^{2}}\|{\bm{\beta}}_{*}\|^{2}+\Big(\alpha_{Z}-\tfrac{\alpha_{A}}{c}\Big)\,\frac{\theta^{2}}{\theta^{2}+c\rho^{2}}\,({\bm{\beta}}_{*}^{\top}{\bm{u}})^{2}\right).

Lastly, replacing
\tilde{\rho}
,
\rho
with
\tilde{\tau}
,
\tau
and using
d/n\to c
yield the detailed expressions in Theorem
5
, up to simple algebra (rearranging terms and simplifying the fractions).

Appendix E
Probability Lemmas

Proposition 2
.

If
{\bm{u}},{\bm{v}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d}
are fixed unit norm vector and
{\bm{A}}\in\mathbb{R}^{d\times n}
is a Gaussian matrix with i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
entries. If
d>n
, then we have that

\mathbb{E}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{v}})^{2}]=\frac{n}{d(d+2)}\left[({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}(n+2)+\frac{(1-({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2})(d-n)}{d-1}\right]=\frac{1}{c^{2}}({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}+o(1),

\mathrm{Var}\left({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}{\bm{v}})^{2}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

Proof.

Let
{\bm{P}}:={\bm{A}}{\bm{A}}^{\dagger}
. This is the orthogonal projection matrix onto the column space of
{\bm{A}}
, denoted
C({\bm{A}})=\text{Range}({\bm{A}})
. The subspace
C({\bm{A}})
is an
n
-dimensional subspace of
\mathbb{R}^{d}
. Because the entries
A_{ij}
are i.i.d.
\mathcal{N}(0,1)
, the distribution of the random subspace
C({\bm{A}})
is isotropic (or rotationally invariant). Consequently, the distribution of the random projection matrix
{\bm{P}}
is also rotationally invariant. That is, for any fixed
d\times d
orthogonal matrix
{\bm{Q}}
, the distribution of
{\bm{Q}}{\bm{P}}{\bm{Q}}^{\top}
is the same as the distribution of
{\bm{P}}
.

We are interested in
\mathbb{E}[({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}{\bm{v}})^{2}]
. Let
\theta
be the angle between
{\bm{u}}
and
{\bm{v}}
, such that
\cos(\theta)={\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}}
(since they are unit vectors). Due to the rotational invariance of the distribution of
{\bm{P}}
, we can choose an orthonormal basis without loss of generality. Let
{\bm{Q}}
be an orthogonal matrix such that
{\bm{u}}^{\prime}={\bm{Q}}{\bm{u}}={\bm{e}}_{1}=(1,0,\dots,0)^{\top}
and
{\bm{v}}^{\prime}={\bm{Q}}{\bm{v}}
lies in the span of
{\bm{e}}_{1}
and
{\bm{e}}_{2}
. Specifically,
{\bm{v}}^{\prime}=\cos(\theta){\bm{e}}_{1}+\sin(\theta){\bm{e}}_{2}
. Let
{\bm{P}}^{\prime}={\bm{Q}}{\bm{P}}{\bm{Q}}^{\top}
.
{\bm{P}}^{\prime}
has the same distribution as
{\bm{P}}
. Then,

{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}{\bm{v}}=({\bm{Q}}^{\top}{\bm{u}}^{\prime})^{\top}{\bm{P}}({\bm{Q}}^{\top}{\bm{v}}^{\prime})=({\bm{u}}^{\prime})^{\top}({\bm{Q}}{\bm{P}}{\bm{Q}}^{\top}){\bm{v}}^{\prime}=({\bm{u}}^{\prime})^{\top}{\bm{P}}^{\prime}{\bm{v}}^{\prime}

Substituting
{\bm{u}}^{\prime}={\bm{e}}_{1}
and
{\bm{v}}^{\prime}=\cos(\theta){\bm{e}}_{1}+\sin(\theta){\bm{e}}_{2}
:

\displaystyle{\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}{\bm{v}}

\displaystyle={\bm{e}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{P}}^{\prime}(\cos(\theta){\bm{e}}_{1}+\sin(\theta){\bm{e}}_{2})

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)({\bm{e}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{P}}^{\prime}{\bm{e}}_{1})+\sin(\theta)({\bm{e}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{P}}^{\prime}e_{2})

\displaystyle=\cos(\theta)P^{\prime}_{11}+\sin(\theta)P^{\prime}_{12}

where
P^{\prime}_{ij}
are the elements of
{\bm{P}}^{\prime}
. Since
{\bm{P}}^{\prime}
has the same distribution as
{\bm{P}}
, we can drop the prime for calculating expectations involving the elements. Let
{\bm{X}}={\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{P}}{\bm{v}}
. We then need
\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]
.

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[(\cos(\theta)P_{11}+\sin(\theta)P_{12})^{2}]

\displaystyle=\mathbb{E}[\cos^{2}(\theta)P_{11}^{2}+\sin^{2}(\theta)P_{12}^{2}+2\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)P_{11}P_{12}]

\displaystyle=\cos^{2}(\theta)\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]+\sin^{2}(\theta)\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]+2\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\mathbb{E}[P_{11}P_{12}]

Calculation of Moments.
We need to compute
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]
,
\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]
, and
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}P_{12}]
.

Consider a reflection matrix
{\bm{R}}
that maps
{\bm{e}}_{2}
to
-{\bm{e}}_{2}
and leaves other basis vectors unchanged (i.e.,
{\bm{R}}=\text{diag}(1,-1,1,\dots,1)
). Since the distribution of
{\bm{P}}
is isotropic, it is invariant under reflection. Let
{\bm{P}}^{*}={\bm{R}}{\bm{P}}{\bm{R}}^{\top}={\bm{R}}{\bm{P}}{\bm{R}}
.
{\bm{P}}^{*}
has the same distribution as
{\bm{P}}
.
The components are related:

P^{*}_{11}=(RPR)_{11}=R_{11}P_{11}R_{11}=P_{11}

and

P^{*}_{12}=(RPR)_{12}=R_{11}P_{12}R_{22}=(1)P_{12}(-1)=-P_{12}.

Therefore,

\mathbb{E}[P_{11}P_{12}]=\mathbb{E}[P^{*}_{11}P^{*}_{12}]=\mathbb{E}[P_{11}(-P_{12})]=-\mathbb{E}[P_{11}P_{12}].

This implies
2\mathbb{E}[P_{11}P_{12}]=0
, so
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}P_{12}]=0
.

The diagonal element
P_{11}={\bm{e}}_{1}^{\top}{\bm{P}}{\bm{e}}_{1}=\|{\bm{P}}{\bm{e}}_{1}\|_{2}^{2}
represents the squared norm of the projection of the fixed unit vector
{\bm{e}}_{1}
onto the random
n
-dimensional subspace
C({\bm{A}})
. This variable follows a Beta distribution:

P_{11}\sim\text{Beta}\left(\frac{n}{2},\frac{d-n}{2}\right)

The mean and variance of a
\text{Beta}(\alpha,\beta)
distribution are
\frac{\alpha}{\alpha+\beta}
and
\frac{\alpha\beta}{(\alpha+\beta)^{2}(\alpha+\beta+1)}
, respectively.
Here,
\alpha=n/2
and
\beta=(d-n)/2
, so
\alpha+\beta=d/2
.

\mathbb{E}[P_{11}]=\frac{n/2}{d/2}=\frac{n}{d}

Next

\mathrm{Var}(P_{11})=\frac{(n/2)((d-n)/2)}{(d/2)^{2}(d/2+1)}=\frac{n(d-n)/4}{(d^{2}/4)((d+2)/2)}=\frac{n(d-n)\cdot 8}{4d^{2}(d+2)}=\frac{2n(d-n)}{d^{2}(d+2)}

Now we find
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]
using
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]=\mathrm{Var}(P_{11})+(\mathbb{E}[P_{11}])^{2}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]

\displaystyle=\frac{2n(d-n)}{d^{2}(d+2)}+\left(\frac{n}{d}\right)^{2}

\displaystyle=\frac{2n(d-n)+n^{2}(d+2)}{d^{2}(d+2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2nd-2n^{2}+n^{2}d+2n^{2}}{d^{2}(d+2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{2nd+n^{2}d}{d^{2}(d+2)}

\displaystyle=\frac{n(n+2)}{d(d+2)}.

We use the property that
{\bm{P}}
is a projection matrix, so
{\bm{P}}^{2}={\bm{P}}
. The trace is
\Tr({\bm{P}})=n
. Also
\Tr({\bm{P}}^{2})=\Tr({\bm{P}})=n
.
We can write
\Tr({\bm{P}}^{2})=\Tr({\bm{P}}{\bm{P}}^{\top})
since
{\bm{P}}
is symmetric.

\Tr({\bm{P}}^{2})=\sum_{i=1}^{d}\sum_{j=1}^{d}(P_{ij})^{2}

Taking the expectation:

\mathbb{E}[\Tr({\bm{P}}^{2})]=\mathbb{E}\left[\sum_{i,j}P_{ij}^{2}\right]=\sum_{i,j}\mathbb{E}[P_{ij}^{2}]=n

By rotational symmetry,
\mathbb{E}[P_{ii}^{2}]
is the same for all
i
, and
\mathbb{E}[P_{ij}^{2}]
is the same for all
i\neq j
.

\sum_{i=1}^{d}\mathbb{E}[P_{ii}^{2}]+\sum_{i\neq j}\mathbb{E}[P_{ij}^{2}]=n.

There are
d
diagonal terms and
d(d-1)
off-diagonal terms.

d\,\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]+d(d-1)\,\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]=n

Substitute the value for
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]
(assuming
d>1
):

d\left(\frac{n(n+2)}{d(d+2)}\right)+d(d-1)\,\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]=n

\frac{n(n+2)}{d+2}+d(d-1)\,\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]=n

d(d-1)\,\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]=n-\frac{n(n+2)}{d+2}=\frac{n(d+2)-n(n+2)}{d+2}=\frac{nd+2n-n^{2}-2n}{d+2}=\frac{n(d-n)}{d+2}

\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]=\frac{n(d-n)}{d(d-1)(d+2)}

Substitute the moments back into the expression for
\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]
:

\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]=\cos^{2}(\theta)\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]+\sin^{2}(\theta)\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{2}]+2\cos(\theta)\sin(\theta)\cdot 0

Using
\cos(\theta)={\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}}
,
\cos^{2}(\theta)=({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}
, and
\sin^{2}(\theta)=1-\cos^{2}(\theta)=1-({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[(u^{\top}AA^{\dagger}v)^{2}]

\displaystyle=({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}\left(\frac{n(n+2)}{d(d+2)}\right)+(1-({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2})\left(\frac{n(d-n)}{d(d-1)(d+2)}\right)

\displaystyle=\frac{n}{d(d+2)}\left[({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}(n+2)+\frac{(1-({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2})(d-n)}{d-1}\right]

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{c^{2}}({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

Calculation of Variance.
Recall that reflection
{\bm{R}}=\mathrm{diag}(1,-1,1,\dots,1)
implies
{\bm{P}}\stackrel{{\scriptstyle d}}{{=}}{\bm{R}}{\bm{P}}{\bm{R}}
(equal in distribution) and thus
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}P_{12}]=0
, and in general any mixed moment with an odd power of
P_{12}
vanishes.
Therefore, we have the following expansion:

\mathbb{E}[X^{4}]=\cos^{4}\theta\,\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{4}]+6\cos^{2}\theta\sin^{2}\theta\,\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}P_{12}^{2}]+\sin^{4}\theta\,\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{4}].

(31)

We start with
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{4}]
. Since
P_{11}\sim\mathrm{Beta}(\alpha,\beta)
with
\alpha=\frac{n}{2}
,
\beta=\frac{d-n}{2}
. We need the higher moments for the Beta distribution: for
m\geq 1
,

\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{m}]=\frac{\alpha^{(m)}}{(\alpha+\beta)^{(m)}}=\frac{(\frac{n}{2})^{(m)}}{(\frac{d}{2})^{(m)}},\qquad x^{(m)}:=x(x+1)\cdots(x+m-1).

In particular, we have the following third and fourth moments:

\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{3}]=\frac{(\frac{n}{2})^{(3)}}{(\frac{d}{2})^{(3)}}=\frac{1}{c^{3}}+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right),\quad\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{4}]=\frac{(\frac{n}{2})^{(4)}}{(\frac{d}{2})^{(4)}}=\frac{1}{c^{4}}+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

We now move on to
\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}P_{12}^{2}]
.From idempotency,
(P^{2})_{11}=P_{11}
gives the row identity
P_{11}=\sum_{k=1}^{d}P_{1k}^{2}
. Multiplying by
P_{11}^{2}
and taking expectations, we have that

\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{3}]=\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{4}]+\sum_{k=2}^{d}\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}P_{1k}^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{4}]+(d-1)\,\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}P_{12}^{2}].

\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}P_{12}^{2}]=\frac{\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{3}]-\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{4}]}{d-1}=\frac{1}{d-1}\left(\frac{(\frac{n}{2})^{(3)}}{(\frac{d}{2})^{(3)}}-\frac{(\frac{n}{2})^{(4)}}{(\frac{d}{2})^{(4)}}\right)=\frac{1}{d-1}\!\left(\frac{1}{c^{3}}-\frac{1}{c^{4}}+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

We still need to evaluate or upper bound
\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{4}]
. From
P_{11}=\sum_{k=1}^{d}P_{1k}^{2}
we have
\sum_{k=2}^{d}P_{1k}^{2}=P_{11}-P_{11}^{2}
. By Cauchy–Schwarz,

\sum_{k=2}^{d}P_{1k}^{4}=\left(\sum_{k=2}^{d}P_{1k}^{2}\right)^{2}=(P_{11}-P_{11}^{2})^{2}.

Taking expectations, we get:

(d-1)\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{4}]\leq\mathbb{E}[(P_{11}-P_{11}^{2})^{2}]=\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{2}]-2\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{3}]+\mathbb{E}[P_{11}^{4}].

\mathbb{E}[P_{12}^{4}]\leq\frac{1}{d-1}\!\left(\frac{1}{c^{2}}-\frac{2}{c^{3}}+\frac{1}{c^{4}}\right)+O\left(\frac{1}{d^{2}}\right)=O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

We can now plug these expectation bounds into Equation
31
:

\displaystyle\mathbb{E}[X^{4}]

\displaystyle=\cos^{4}\theta\frac{(\frac{n}{2})^{(4)}}{(\frac{d}{2})^{(4)}}+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)6\cos^{2}\theta\sin^{2}\theta\ +O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right)\sin^{4}\theta

\displaystyle=\frac{1}{c^{4}}({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{4}+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

Recall from the prior proof that:

\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]=\cos^{2}\theta\,\frac{n(n+2)}{d(d+2)}+\sin^{2}\theta\,\frac{n(d-n)}{d(d-1)(d+2)}=\frac{1}{c^{2}}({\bm{u}}^{\top}{\bm{v}})^{2}+O\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

Finally, we have that the variance is of order:

\mathrm{Var}(X^{2})=\mathbb{E}[X^{4}]-\big(\mathbb{E}[X^{2}]\big)^{2}=O\!\left(\frac{1}{d}\right).

∎

Lemma 32
.

Let
a\neq 0
be a constant and suppose that
\zeta=a+o(f(n))
as
n\to\infty
. Then,

\frac{1}{\zeta}=\frac{1}{a}+o(f(n)).

Proof.

Write
\zeta=a+r_{n}
with
r_{n}=o(f(n))
. Then

\frac{1}{\zeta}=\frac{1}{a+r_{n}}=\frac{1}{a}\cdot\frac{1}{1+\frac{r_{n}}{a}}.

Using the expansion

\frac{1}{1+u}=1-u+O(u^{2})\quad\text{as }u\to 0,

with
u=r_{n}/a
, we obtain

\frac{1}{\zeta}=\frac{1}{a}\Big(1-\frac{r_{n}}{a}+O\!\big((r_{n}/a)^{2}\big)\Big)=\frac{1}{a}-\frac{r_{n}}{a^{2}}+O(r_{n}^{2}).

Since
r_{n}=o(f(n))
and
f(n)\to 0
, we have
r_{n}^{2}=o(f(n))
. Therefore

\frac{1}{\zeta}=\frac{1}{a}+o\!\big(f(n)\big),

which is the desired expansion.
∎

Lemma 33

(Variance of a reciprocal)
.

Let
X
be a random variable satisfying

E[X]=a>0\quad\text{and}\quad\mathrm{Var}(X)=\sigma^{2}=o(1),

and assume that
X
is bounded away from zero with high probability. That is, there exists
C\in(0,a)
such that

\Pr[X\geq C]=1-o(1)

If there exists an
M
such that

\mathbb{E}\left[X^{-8}\right]\leq M\quad\text{ and }\quad\mathbb{E}\left[\left(X-\mathbb{E}[X]\right)^{4}\right]=O(\sigma^{4})

Then

\mathrm{Var}\left(\frac{1}{X}\right)=\frac{1}{a^{4}}\mathrm{Var}(X)+o\left(\mathrm{Var}(X)\right),

so in particular,
\mathrm{Var}(1/X)=o(1)
.

Proof.

Let
Y:=X-a
. Then

\mathbb{E}\left[Y\right]=0,\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\right]=\sigma^{2},\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]=O\!\left(\sigma^{4}\right).

By Taylor’s theorem with Lagrange remainder for
f(x)=1/x
, there exists
\theta=\theta(X)\in(0,1)
such that

\frac{1}{X}=\frac{1}{a}-\frac{Y}{a^{2}}+Z,\qquad Z:=\frac{Y^{2}}{\left(a+\theta Y\right)^{3}}\ \geq 0.

Write
\Delta:=\frac{1}{X}-\frac{1}{a}=-\frac{Y}{a^{2}}+Z
. Then

\mathrm{Var}\!\left(\frac{1}{X}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta^{2}\right]-\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\right]\right)^{2}.

We will show

\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta^{2}\right]=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{a^{4}}+o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)\quad\text{and}\quad\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\right]\right)^{2}=o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

Let
G:=\{X\geq C\}
and
B:=\{X<C\}
. Since
C<a
and
\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\right]=\sigma^{2}
,
Chebyshev gives the quantitative bound

\Pr\left[B\right]=\Pr\left[\,|Y|\geq a-C\,\right]\leq\frac{\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\right]}{(a-C)^{2}}=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{(a-C)^{2}}=O\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)=o(1).

Second moment
\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta^{2}\right]
.

We split over
G
and
B
.

On
G
.
Since
a+\theta Y=\theta X+(1-\theta)a\geq C
, we have

\left|Z\right|\leq\frac{Y^{2}}{C^{3}},\qquad Z^{2}\leq\frac{Y^{4}}{C^{6}}.

Therefore

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(-\frac{Y}{a^{2}}+Z\right)^{\!2}\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]=\frac{1}{a^{4}}\,\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]-\frac{2}{a^{2}}\,\mathbb{E}\left[YZ\,\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{2}\mathbf{1}_{G}\right].

We bound each term as follows.

\mathbb{E}\left[Z^{2}\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]\leq\frac{1}{C^{6}}\,\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]=O\!\left(\sigma^{4}\right),

and, using
\mathbf{1}_{G}\leq 1
and Lyapunov/monotonicity of
L^{p}
norms,

\mathbb{E}\left[\left|YZ\right|\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]\leq\frac{1}{C^{3}}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\left|Y\right|^{3}\right]\leq\frac{1}{C^{3}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]\right)^{3/4}=O\!\left(\sigma^{3}\right)=o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

Moreover,

\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]=\sigma^{2}-\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\mathbf{1}_{B}\right],\qquad\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}\Pr\left[B\right]^{1/2}=O\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)\Pr\left[B\right]^{1/2}=o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

Hence

\mathbb{E}\left[\left(-\frac{Y}{a^{2}}+Z\right)^{\!2}\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{a^{4}}+o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

On
B
.
Using the algebraic identity

\left(\frac{1}{X}-\frac{1}{a}\right)^{2}=\frac{Y^{2}}{a^{2}X^{2}},

Cauchy–Schwarz and Hölder (with exponents
2,2
) give

\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta^{2}\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]=\frac{1}{a^{2}}\,\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y^{2}}{X^{2}}\,\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]\leq\frac{1}{a^{2}}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X^{-4}\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq\frac{1}{a^{2}}\,O\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X^{-8}\right]\right)^{1/4}\Pr\left[B\right]^{1/4}.

Under the lemma’s assumption
\mathbb{E}\left[X^{-8}\right]\leq M
, we get

\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta^{2}\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]=O\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)\Pr\left[B\right]^{1/4}=o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

Combining the
G
and
B
parts,

\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta^{2}\right]=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{a^{4}}+o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

Mean correction
\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\right]\right)^{2}
.

Since
\mathbb{E}\left[Y\right]=0
, we have

\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[Z\right]=\mathbb{E}\left[Z\,\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]+\mathbb{E}\left[Z\,\mathbf{1}_{B}\right].

On
G
,
Z\leq Y^{2}/C^{3}
, so

\mathbb{E}\left[Z\,\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]\leq\frac{1}{C^{3}}\,\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{2}\mathbf{1}_{G}\right]\leq\frac{1}{C^{3}}\,\sigma^{2}.

On
B
, The inequality

Z=\frac{Y^{2}}{a+\theta Y)^{3}}\leq\frac{X^{2}}{Y^{3}}

holds on set
B
because on this set as
X<a
, meaning the point
a+\theta Y
lies between
X
and
a
, so
a+\theta Y>X
. Thus, using Cauchy–Schwarz and Hölder,

\mathbb{E}\left[Z\,\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]\leq\mathbb{E}\left[\frac{Y^{2}}{X^{3}}\,\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]\leq\left(\mathbb{E}\left[Y^{4}\right]\right)^{1/2}\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X^{-6}\mathbf{1}_{B}\right]\right)^{1/2}\leq O\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)\left(\mathbb{E}\left[X^{-12}\right]\right)^{1/4}\Pr\left[B\right]^{1/4}=o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

Thus
\left|\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\right]\right|=O\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)
and therefore

\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\right]\right)^{2}=O\!\left(\sigma^{4}\right)=o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right).

Putting the two steps together,

\mathrm{Var}\!\left(\frac{1}{X}\right)=\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta^{2}\right]-\left(\mathbb{E}\left[\Delta\right]\right)^{2}=\frac{\sigma^{2}}{a^{4}}+o\!\left(\sigma^{2}\right)=\frac{1}{a^{4}}\mathrm{Var}\!\left(X\right)+o\!\left(\mathrm{Var}\!\left(X\right)\right).

∎

Lemma 34

(Variance of a sum)
.

Let
A
and
B
be any random variables with finite variances
V(A)=\mathrm{Var}(A)
and
V(B)=\mathrm{Var}(B)
. Then,

\mathrm{Var}(A+B)\leq\left(\sqrt{V(A)}+\sqrt{V(B)}\right)^{2}.

Proof.

Recall that

\mathrm{Var}(A+B)=\mathrm{Var}(A)+\mathrm{Var}(B)+2\,\operatorname{Cov}(A,B).

By the Cauchy–Schwarz inequality, we have

\left|\operatorname{Cov}(A,B)\right|\leq\sqrt{V(A)V(B)}.

Thus,

\mathrm{Var}(A+B)\leq V(A)+V(B)+2\sqrt{V(A)V(B)}=\left(\sqrt{V(A)}+\sqrt{V(B)}\right)^{2}.

∎

Lemma 35

(Variance of one product)
.

Let
A,B
